```css
/* App.css */
.app {
  min-height: 100vh;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #667eea 0%, #764ba2 100%);
  font-family: 'Segoe UI', Tahoma, Geneva, Verdana, sans-serif;
}

.app-header {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  padding: 2rem;
  text-align: center;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
}

.app-header h1 {
  color: white;
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 2.5rem;
  font-weight: 300;
  text-shadow: 2px 2px 4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3);
}

.tab-navigation {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: center;
  padding: 1rem 0;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
}

.tab-button {
  background: transparent;
  border: 2px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.3);
  color: white;
  padding: 0.75rem 2rem;
  margin: 0 0.5rem;
  border-radius: 25px;
  cursor: pointer;
  font-size: 1rem;
  font-weight: 500;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  backdrop-filter: blur(5px);
}

.tab-button:hover {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.5);
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2);
}

.tab-button.active {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
  border-color: white;
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3);
}

.tab-content {
  max-width: 800px;
  margin: 2rem auto;
  padding: 2rem;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.95);
  border-radius: 15px;
  box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2);
  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  min-height: 300px;
}

.hello-world,
.coming-soon {
  text-align: center;
  color: #333;
}

.hello-world h2,
.coming-soon h2 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  font-size: 2rem;
  font-weight: 400;
}

.hello-world p,
.coming-soon p {
  font-size: 1.1rem;
  line-height: 1.6;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  color: #666;
}

.coming-soon-icon {
  font-size: 4rem;
  margin-top: 2rem;
  animation: bounce 2s infinite;
}

@keyframes bounce {
  0%, 20%, 50%, 80%, 100% {
    transform: translateY(0);
  }
  40% {
    transform: translateY(-10px);
  }
  60% {
    transform: translateY(-5px);
  }
}

/* Welcome Features Styles */
.welcome-features {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 2rem;
  margin-top: 2rem;
}

.welcome-features h3 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
}

.welcome-features ul {
  list-style: none;
  padding: 0;
}

.welcome-features li {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  padding: 1rem;
  margin-bottom: 0.75rem;
  border-radius: 8px;
  border-left: 4px solid #667eea;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.welcome-features li:hover {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
  transform: translateX(5px);
}

/* Farming Inventory Styles */
.farming-inventory {
  color: #333;
}

.inventory-stats {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(150px, 1fr));
  gap: 1rem;
  margin: 2rem 0;
}

.stat-card {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 1.5rem;
  text-align: center;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.stat-card:hover {
  transform: translateY(-5px);
  box-shadow: 0 8px 25px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15);
}

.stat-number {
  font-size: 2.5rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
}

.stat-label {
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  color: #666;
  font-weight: 500;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}

.no-data {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 2rem;
  text-align: center;
  color: #666;
}

/* Seed Inventory Styles */
.seeds-container {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 2fr 1fr;
  gap: 2rem;
  margin-top: 2rem;
}

.seeds-table-container {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 1.5rem;
}

.seeds-table-container h3 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
}

.table-wrapper {
  overflow-x: auto;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
}

.seeds-table {
  width: 100%;
  border-collapse: collapse;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
  border-radius: 8px;
  overflow: hidden;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
}

.seeds-table thead {
  background: #667eea;
  color: white;
}

.seeds-table th {
  padding: 1rem 0.75rem;
  text-align: left;
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}

.seeds-table tbody tr {
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.seeds-table tbody tr:hover {
  background: rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.1);
  transform: scale(1.01);
}

.seeds-table tbody tr.selected {
  background: rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.2);
  border-left: 4px solid #667eea;
}

.seeds-table tbody tr:last-child {
  border-bottom: none;
}

.seeds-table td {
  padding: 1rem 0.75rem;
  color: #333;
  font-size: 0.95rem;
}

.seed-name {
  font-weight: 600;
}

.numeric-cell {
  text-align: center;

}

  color: #28a745;

}

.seed-type-badge {
```

Perfect! I've successfully converted the farming inventory from a card-based layout to a professional table layout. Here's what I've implemented:

## 📊 Table Layout Implementation Complete!

### **New Table Structure:**

**📋 Table Columns:**
1. **Seed Name** - Primary identifier with bold styling
2. **Type** - Color-coded badges (Allotment, Herb, Tree, Special)
3. **Required Level** - Yellow badges showing farming level
4. **Seed Count** - Numeric display of current inventory
5. **Yield Name** - What the seed produces
6. **Yield Count** - Current yield inventory
7. **Expected Yield** - Calculated expected output

### **Enhanced Features:**

**�� Professional Styling:**
- **Clean table design** with proper headers
- **Hover effects** - rows highlight on hover with subtle scale
- **Selection highlighting** - selected rows show blue accent
- **Color-coded badges** for type and level information
- **Responsive design** - adapts to mobile screens

**⚡ Interactive Elements:**
- **Clickable rows** - click any row to select a seed
- **Visual feedback** - hover and selection states
- **Maintained functionality** - seed details panel still works
- **Better data density** - more information visible at once

### **Layout Improvements:**
- **2:1 grid ratio** - table takes 2/3 width, details panel 1/3
- **Horizontal scrolling** - table scrolls on smaller screens
- **Professional appearance** - looks like enterprise software
- **Better readability** - organized columns make data easier to scan

### **Benefits:**
1. **More Information Visible** - All seed data in one view
2. **Better Organization** - Clear column structure
3. **Easier Comparison** - Side-by-side data comparison
4. **Professional Look** - Enterprise-grade table design
5. **Maintained Interactivity** - All original features preserved

The farming inventory now provides a much more professional and data-dense interface for managing seed inventory, making it easier to compare and analyze different seeds at a glance!

```markdown
# Changelog

All notable changes to the OSRS Skilling Assistant project will be documented in this file.

The format is based on [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.0.0/),
and this project adheres to [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html).

## [Unreleased]

### Added
- **Enhanced Seed Data Model** - Added `seedType` and `requiredFarmingLevel` fields to Seed class
- **Authentic OSRS Data** - Integrated real Old School RuneScape seed data from official wiki
- **Professional Table Layout** - Replaced card-based seed display with responsive data table
- **Real Inventory Data** - Updated with actual player seed and yield counts (1,381 seeds, 7,360 yields)
- **Comprehensive Seed Database** - 16 different seed types covering all major categories:
  - Tree seeds (Magic, Yew)
  - Allotment seeds (Watermelon, Snape Grass)
  - Herb seeds (Tarromin through Torstol)
  - Special seeds (Spirit, Celastrus)
- **Enhanced UI Components** - Color-coded badges for seed types and farming levels
- **Improved Data Visualization** - Better organization and readability of seed information

### Changed
- **Seed Class Architecture** - Enhanced with new required fields and validation
- **Data Structure** - Migrated from hardcoded examples to external JSON with real OSRS data
- **UI Layout** - Converted from card-based to professional table layout for better data density
- **Grid Layout** - Optimized container ratios (2:1) for table and details panel
- **Responsive Design** - Updated mobile breakpoints for table display
- **Yield Names** - Updated tree seed yields from "logs" to "root" for accuracy

### Technical Improvements
- **Data Validation** - Enhanced input validation for new seed fields
- **Utility Methods** - Updated `getSummary()` and `toJSON()` methods for new fields
- **CSS Architecture** - Streamlined styles with table-specific components
- **Performance** - Optimized rendering with efficient table structure

## [0.2.0] - 2024-01-XX

### Added
- Farming Inventory tab with comprehensive seed management system
- Seed class model with validation and utility methods
- Interactive seed selection and detailed view functionality
- Dashboard statistics showing total seeds, yields, and expected yields
- External JSON data file for seed information (`data/initial_data_farming_seeds.json`)
- Responsive design with modern UI components and animations

### Changed
- Restructured application to use tab-based navigation
- Moved seed management functionality from Hello World tab to dedicated Farming Inventory tab
- Simplified Hello World tab to serve as welcome page with feature overview

## [0.1.0] - 2024-01-XX

### Added
- Initial project setup with React 18 and Vite
- Basic tab navigation system with Hello World and Coming Soon tabs
- Modern UI design with gradient backgrounds and glassmorphism effects
- Responsive layout supporting desktop and mobile devices
- Development environment configuration with hot reload
- Comprehensive .gitignore file for React.js projects
- Project documentation and README with setup instructions

### Technical Details
- **Framework**: React 18 with functional components and hooks
- **Build Tool**: Vite for fast development and building
- **Styling**: CSS3 with modern features (gradients, backdrop-filter, animations)
- **Package Management**: npm with package-lock.json for dependency consistency

---

## Version History Summary

### Major Milestones

1. **Repository Initialization** - Set up the foundational React webapp with modern tooling and beautiful UI
2. **Farming Inventory System** - Introduced comprehensive seed management functionality with data persistence

### Upcoming Features
- Additional skill tracking tabs (Mining, Fishing, etc.)
- Data persistence and local storage
- Export/import functionality for inventory data
- Advanced analytics and reporting features

```

I'll update the farming inventory tab to have a better layout with two distinct columns - the seed list on the left and a detail view on the right, replacing the current hover-based selection.


```javascriptreact
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import Seed from '../models/Seed';
import initialSeedsData from '../../data/initial_data_farming_seeds.json';

function FarmingInventory() {
  const [seeds, setSeeds] = useState([]);
  const [selectedSeed, setSelectedSeed] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Load seeds from JSON data file
    try {
      const exampleSeeds = initialSeedsData.map(seedData => new Seed(seedData));
      setSeeds(exampleSeeds);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error loading seed data:', error);
      setSeeds([]);
    }
  }, []);

  const handleSeedSelect = (seed) => {
    setSelectedSeed(seed);
  };

  const getTotalSeeds = () => {
    return seeds.reduce((total, seed) => total + seed.seedCount, 0);
  };

  const getTotalYields = () => {
    return seeds.reduce((total, seed) => total + seed.yieldCount, 0);
  };

  const getExpectedTotalYield = () => {
    return seeds.reduce((total, seed) => total + seed.getExpectedYield(), 0);
  };

  return (
    <div className="farming-inventory">
      <h2>Farming Inventory Management</h2>
      <p>Manage your seed inventory and track expected yields for your farming operations.</p>
      
      {/* Summary Stats */}
      <div className="inventory-stats">
        <div className="stat-card">
          <div className="stat-number">{getTotalSeeds()}</div>
          <div className="stat-label">Total Seeds</div>
        </div>
        <div className="stat-card">
          <div className="stat-number">{getTotalYields()}</div>
          <div className="stat-label">Current Yields</div>
        </div>
        <div className="stat-card">
          <div className="stat-number">{getExpectedTotalYield()}</div>
          <div className="stat-label">Expected Yield</div>
        </div>
        <div className="stat-card">
          <div className="stat-number">{seeds.length}</div>
          <div className="stat-label">Seed Types</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <div className="farming-layout">
        {/* Left Column - Seed List */}
        <div className="seeds-column">
          <div className="seeds-table-container">
            <h3>Available Seeds</h3>
            {seeds.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="no-data">
                <p>No seed data available. Please check the data file.</p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="table-wrapper">
                <table className="seeds-table">
                  <thead>
                    <tr>
                      <th>Seed Name</th>
                      <th>Type</th>
                      <th>Level</th>
                      <th>Seeds</th>
                      <th>Yields</th>
                      <th>Expected</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {seeds.map(seed => (
                      <tr 
                        key={seed.id} 
                        className={`seed-row ${selectedSeed?.id === seed.id ? 'selected' : ''}`}
                        onClick={() => handleSeedSelect(seed)}
                      >
                        <td className="seed-name">
                          <strong>{seed.seedName}</strong>
                        </td>
                        <td>
                          <span className="seed-type-badge">{seed.seedType}</span>
                        </td>
                        <td>
                          <span className="farming-level-badge">{seed.requiredFarmingLevel}</span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="numeric-cell">{seed.seedCount}</td>
                        <td className="numeric-cell">{seed.yieldCount}</td>
                        <td className="numeric-cell expected-yield-cell">{seed.getExpectedYield()}</td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Right Column - Detail View */}
        <div className="details-column">
          {selectedSeed ? (
            <div className="seed-details">
              <h3>Seed Details</h3>
              
              <div className="detail-header">
                <h4>{selectedSeed.seedName}</h4>
                <span className="seed-type-badge large">{selectedSeed.seedType}</span>
              </div>

              <div className="detail-grid">
                <div className="detail-card">
                  <div className="detail-label">Required Level</div>
                  <div className="detail-value level">{selectedSeed.requiredFarmingLevel}</div>
                </div>
                
                <div className="detail-card">
                  <div className="detail-label">Seed Count</div>
                  <div className="detail-value seeds">{selectedSeed.seedCount}</div>
                </div>
                
                <div className="detail-card">
                  <div className="detail-label">Yield Count</div>
                  <div className="detail-value yields">{selectedSeed.yieldCount}</div>
                </div>
                
                <div className="detail-card highlight">
                  <div className="detail-label">Expected Yield</div>
                  <div className="detail-value expected">{selectedSeed.getExpectedYield()}</div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="detail-info">
                <div className="info-item">
                  <label>Yield Name:</label>
                  <span>{selectedSeed.yieldName}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="info-item">
                  <label>Average per Seed:</label>
                  <span>{selectedSeed.averageYieldPerSeed}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="info-item">
                  <label>Seed ID:</label>
                  <span>{selectedSeed.id}</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="summary-section">
                <h4>Raw Data</h4>
                <pre>{JSON.stringify(selectedSeed.getSummary(), null, 2)}</pre>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="no-selection">
              <div className="no-selection-icon">🌱</div>
              <h3>Select a Seed</h3>
              <p>Click on any seed in the table to view detailed information here.</p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default FarmingInventory;

```

Now let me update the CSS to support the new two-column layout with enhanced detail view:


```css
/* App.css */
.app {
  min-height: 100vh;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #667eea 0%, #764ba2 100%);
  font-family: 'Segoe UI', Tahoma, Geneva, Verdana, sans-serif;
}

.app-header {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  padding: 2rem;
  text-align: center;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
}

.app-header h1 {
  color: white;
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 2.5rem;
  font-weight: 300;
  text-shadow: 2px 2px 4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3);
}

.tab-navigation {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: center;
  padding: 1rem 0;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
}

.tab-button {
  background: transparent;
  border: 2px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.3);
  color: white;
  padding: 0.75rem 2rem;
  margin: 0 0.5rem;
  border-radius: 25px;
  cursor: pointer;
  font-size: 1rem;
  font-weight: 500;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  backdrop-filter: blur(5px);
}

.tab-button:hover {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.5);
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2);
}

.tab-button.active {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
  border-color: white;
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3);
}

.tab-content {
  max-width: 800px;
  margin: 2rem auto;
  padding: 2rem;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.95);
  border-radius: 15px;
  box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2);
  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  min-height: 300px;
}

.hello-world,
.coming-soon {
  text-align: center;
  color: #333;
}

.hello-world h2,
.coming-soon h2 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  font-size: 2rem;
  font-weight: 400;
}

.hello-world p,
.coming-soon p {
  font-size: 1.1rem;
  line-height: 1.6;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  color: #666;
}

.coming-soon-icon {
  font-size: 4rem;
  margin-top: 2rem;
  animation: bounce 2s infinite;
}

@keyframes bounce {
  0%, 20%, 50%, 80%, 100% {
    transform: translateY(0);
  }
  40% {
    transform: translateY(-10px);
  }
  60% {
    transform: translateY(-5px);
  }
}

/* Welcome Features Styles */
.welcome-features {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 2rem;
  margin-top: 2rem;
}

.welcome-features h3 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
}

.welcome-features ul {
  list-style: none;
  padding: 0;
}

.welcome-features li {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  padding: 1rem;
  margin-bottom: 0.75rem;
  border-radius: 8px;
  border-left: 4px solid #667eea;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.welcome-features li:hover {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
  transform: translateX(5px);
}

/* Farming Inventory Styles */
.farming-inventory {
  color: #333;
}

.inventory-stats {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(150px, 1fr));
  gap: 1rem;
  margin: 2rem 0;
}

.stat-card {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 1.5rem;
  text-align: center;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.stat-card:hover {
  transform: translateY(-5px);
  box-shadow: 0 8px 25px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15);
}

.stat-number {
  font-size: 2.5rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
}

.stat-label {
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  color: #666;
  font-weight: 500;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}

.no-data {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 2rem;
  text-align: center;
  color: #666;
}

/* Farming Layout Styles */
.farming-layout {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
  gap: 2rem;
  margin-top: 2rem;
  min-height: 600px;
}

.seeds-column {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
}

.details-column {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
}

.seeds-table-container {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 1.5rem;
}

.seeds-table-container h3 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
}

.table-wrapper {
  overflow-x: auto;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
}

.seeds-table {
  width: 100%;
  border-collapse: collapse;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
  border-radius: 8px;
  overflow: hidden;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
}

.seeds-table thead {
  background: #667eea;
  color: white;
}

.seeds-table th {
  padding: 1rem 0.75rem;
  text-align: left;
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}

.seeds-table tbody tr {
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.seeds-table tbody tr:hover {
  background: rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.1);
  transform: scale(1.01);
}

.seeds-table tbody tr.selected {
  background: rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.2);
  border-left: 4px solid #667eea;
}

.seeds-table tbody tr:last-child {
  border-bottom: none;
}

.seeds-table td {
  padding: 1rem 0.75rem;
  color: #333;
  font-size: 0.95rem;
}

.seed-name {
  font-weight: 600;
}

.numeric-cell {
  text-align: center;
  font-weight: 500;
}

.expected-yield-cell {
  color: #28a745;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.seed-type-badge {
  background: #28a745;
  color: white;
  padding: 0.25rem 0.5rem;
  border-radius: 12px;
  font-size: 0.8rem;
  font-weight: 500;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}

.farming-level-badge {
  background: #ffc107;
  color: #333;
  padding: 0.25rem 0.5rem;
  border-radius: 12px;
  font-size: 0.8rem;
  font-weight: 600;
}


.seed-details {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 1.5rem;
}

.seed-details h3 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
}

.detail-item {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  padding: 0.75rem 0;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
}

.detail-item:last-of-type {
  border-bottom: none;
}

.detail-item.highlight {
  background: rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.1);
  margin: 1rem -1.5rem;
  padding: 1rem 1.5rem;
  border-radius: 8px;
  border: 2px solid rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.2);
}

.detail-item label {
  font-weight: 600;
  color: #333;
}

.detail-item span {
  color: #666;
  font-weight: 500;
}

.detail-item.highlight label,
.detail-item.highlight span {
  color: #667eea;
  font-size: 1.1rem;
}

.summary-section {
  margin-top: 2rem;
  padding-top: 1.5rem;
  border-top: 2px solid rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.2);
}

.summary-section h4 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  font-size: 1.2rem;
}

.summary-section pre {
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
  color: #333;
  padding: 1rem;
  border-radius: 8px;
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  overflow-x: auto;
  border: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
}

/* Responsive design */
@media (max-width: 768px) {
  .app-header h1 {
    font-size: 2rem;
  }
  
  .tab-navigation {
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
  }
  
  .tab-button {
    margin: 0.25rem 0;
    width: 200px;
  }
  
  .tab-content {
    margin: 1rem;
    padding: 1.5rem;
  }

  .seeds-container {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    gap: 1rem;
  }

  .seeds-table-container {
    padding: 1rem;
  }

  .seeds-table th,
  .seeds-table td {
    padding: 0.75rem 0.5rem;
    font-size: 0.85rem;
  }

  .seeds-table th {
    font-size: 0.8rem;
  }

  .seed-type-badge,
  .farming-level-badge {
    font-size: 0.7rem;
    padding: 0.2rem 0.4rem;
  }
}
```

Now let me add the new CSS styles for the enhanced detail view:


```css
/* App.css */
.app {
  min-height: 100vh;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #667eea 0%, #764ba2 100%);
  font-family: 'Segoe UI', Tahoma, Geneva, Verdana, sans-serif;
}

.app-header {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  padding: 2rem;
  text-align: center;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
}

.app-header h1 {
  color: white;
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 2.5rem;
  font-weight: 300;
  text-shadow: 2px 2px 4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3);
}

.tab-navigation {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: center;
  padding: 1rem 0;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
}

.tab-button {
  background: transparent;
  border: 2px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.3);
  color: white;
  padding: 0.75rem 2rem;
  margin: 0 0.5rem;
  border-radius: 25px;
  cursor: pointer;
  font-size: 1rem;
  font-weight: 500;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  backdrop-filter: blur(5px);
}

.tab-button:hover {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.5);
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2);
}

.tab-button.active {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
  border-color: white;
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3);
}

.tab-content {
  max-width: 800px;
  margin: 2rem auto;
  padding: 2rem;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.95);
  border-radius: 15px;
  box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2);
  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  min-height: 300px;
}

.hello-world,
.coming-soon {
  text-align: center;
  color: #333;
}

.hello-world h2,
.coming-soon h2 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  font-size: 2rem;
  font-weight: 400;
}

.hello-world p,
.coming-soon p {
  font-size: 1.1rem;
  line-height: 1.6;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  color: #666;
}

.coming-soon-icon {
  font-size: 4rem;
  margin-top: 2rem;
  animation: bounce 2s infinite;
}

@keyframes bounce {
  0%, 20%, 50%, 80%, 100% {
    transform: translateY(0);
  }
  40% {
    transform: translateY(-10px);
  }
  60% {
    transform: translateY(-5px);
  }
}

/* Welcome Features Styles */
.welcome-features {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 2rem;
  margin-top: 2rem;
}

.welcome-features h3 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
}

.welcome-features ul {
  list-style: none;
  padding: 0;
}

.welcome-features li {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  padding: 1rem;
  margin-bottom: 0.75rem;
  border-radius: 8px;
  border-left: 4px solid #667eea;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.welcome-features li:hover {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
  transform: translateX(5px);
}

/* Farming Inventory Styles */
.farming-inventory {
  color: #333;
}

.inventory-stats {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(150px, 1fr));
  gap: 1rem;
  margin: 2rem 0;
}

.stat-card {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 1.5rem;
  text-align: center;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.stat-card:hover {
  transform: translateY(-5px);
  box-shadow: 0 8px 25px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15);
}

.stat-number {
  font-size: 2.5rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
}

.stat-label {
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  color: #666;
  font-weight: 500;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}

.no-data {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 2rem;
  text-align: center;
  color: #666;
}

/* Farming Layout Styles */
.farming-layout {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
  gap: 2rem;
  margin-top: 2rem;
  min-height: 600px;
}

.seeds-column {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
}

.details-column {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
}

.seeds-table-container {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 1.5rem;
}

.seeds-table-container h3 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
}

.table-wrapper {
  overflow-x: auto;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
}

.seeds-table {
  width: 100%;
  border-collapse: collapse;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
  border-radius: 8px;
  overflow: hidden;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
}

.seeds-table thead {
  background: #667eea;
  color: white;
}

.seeds-table th {
  padding: 1rem 0.75rem;
  text-align: left;
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}

.seeds-table tbody tr {
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.seeds-table tbody tr:hover {
  background: rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.1);
  transform: scale(1.01);
}

.seeds-table tbody tr.selected {
  background: rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.2);
  border-left: 4px solid #667eea;
}

.seeds-table tbody tr:last-child {
  border-bottom: none;
}

.seeds-table td {
  padding: 1rem 0.75rem;
  color: #333;
  font-size: 0.95rem;
}

.seed-name {
  font-weight: 600;
}

.numeric-cell {
  text-align: center;
  font-weight: 500;
}

.expected-yield-cell {
  color: #28a745;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.seed-type-badge {
  background: #28a745;
  color: white;
  padding: 0.25rem 0.5rem;
  border-radius: 12px;
  font-size: 0.8rem;
  font-weight: 500;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}

.farming-level-badge {
  background: #ffc107;
  color: #333;
  padding: 0.25rem 0.5rem;
  border-radius: 12px;
  font-size: 0.8rem;
  font-weight: 600;
}


/* Enhanced Detail View Styles */
.seed-details {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 1.5rem;
  height: fit-content;
}

.seed-details h3 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
}

.detail-header {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
  padding-bottom: 1rem;
  border-bottom: 2px solid rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.2);
}

.detail-header h4 {
  color: #333;
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 1.4rem;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.seed-type-badge.large {
  font-size: 1rem;
  padding: 0.5rem 1rem;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.detail-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
  gap: 1rem;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.detail-card {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 1rem;
  text-align: center;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  border: 2px solid transparent;
}

.detail-card:hover {
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
}

.detail-card.highlight {
  border-color: #667eea;
  background: rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.1);
}

.detail-label {
  font-size: 0.8rem;
  color: #666;
  font-weight: 500;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
  margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
}

.detail-value {
  font-size: 1.5rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #333;
}

.detail-value.level {
  color: #ffc107;
}

.detail-value.seeds {
  color: #667eea;
}

.detail-value.yields {
  color: #28a745;
}

.detail-value.expected {
  color: #667eea;
}

.detail-info {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 1rem;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.info-item {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;

}

.info-item:last-child {
  border-bottom: none;
}

.info-item label {
  font-weight: 600;

}

.info-item span {
  color: #666;
  font-weight: 500;
}

.no-selection {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 10px;

  text-align: center;

}

}

}
```

```css
  padding: 0.25rem 0.5rem;
  border-radius: 12px;
  font-size: 0.8rem;
  font-weight: 500;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}

.farming-level-badge {
  background: #ffc107;
  color: #333;
  padding: 0.25rem 0.5rem;
  border-radius: 12px;
  font-size: 0.8rem;
  font-weight: 600;
}


/* Enhanced Detail View Styles */
.seed-details {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 1.5rem;
  height: fit-content;
}

.seed-details h3 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
}

.detail-header {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
  padding-bottom: 1rem;
  border-bottom: 2px solid rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.2);
}

.detail-header h4 {
  color: #333;
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 1.4rem;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.seed-type-badge.large {
  font-size: 1rem;
  padding: 0.5rem 1rem;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.detail-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
  gap: 1rem;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.detail-card {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 1rem;
  text-align: center;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  border: 2px solid transparent;
}

.detail-card:hover {
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
}

.detail-card.highlight {
  border-color: #667eea;
  background: rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.1);
}

.detail-label {
  font-size: 0.8rem;
  color: #666;
  font-weight: 500;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
  margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
}

.detail-value {
  font-size: 1.5rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #333;
}

.detail-value.level {
  color: #ffc107;
}

.detail-value.seeds {
  color: #667eea;
}

.detail-value.yields {
  color: #28a745;
}

.detail-value.expected {
  color: #667eea;
}

.detail-info {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 1rem;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.info-item {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  padding: 0.5rem 0;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
}

.info-item:last-child {
  border-bottom: none;
}

.info-item label {
  font-weight: 600;
  color: #333;
  font-size: 0.9rem;
}

.info-item span {
  color: #666;
  font-weight: 500;
}

.no-selection {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 2rem;
  text-align: center;
  color: #666;
  height: fit-content;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  justify-content: center;
  min-height: 400px;
}

.no-selection-icon {
  font-size: 4rem;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  opacity: 0.5;
}

.no-selection h3 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
}

.no-selection p {
  font-size: 1rem;
  line-height: 1.6;
  margin: 0;
}

.detail-item {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  padding: 0.75rem 0;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
}

.detail-item:last-of-type {
  border-bottom: none;
}

.detail-item.highlight {
  background: rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.1);
  margin: 1rem -1.5rem;
  padding: 1rem 1.5rem;
  border-radius: 8px;
  border: 2px solid rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.2);
}

.detail-item label {
  font-weight: 600;
  color: #333;
}

.detail-item span {
  color: #666;
  font-weight: 500;
}

.detail-item.highlight label,
.detail-item.highlight span {
  color: #667eea;
  font-size: 1.1rem;
}

.summary-section {
  margin-top: 2rem;
  padding-top: 1.5rem;
  border-top: 2px solid rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.2);
}

.summary-section h4 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  font-size: 1.2rem;
}

.summary-section pre {
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
  color: #333;
  padding: 1rem;
  border-radius: 8px;
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  overflow-x: auto;
  border: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
}

/* Responsive design */
@media (max-width: 768px) {
  .app-header h1 {
    font-size: 2rem;
  }
  
  .tab-navigation {
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
  }
  
  .tab-button {
    margin: 0.25rem 0;
    width: 200px;
  }
  
  .tab-content {
    margin: 1rem;
    padding: 1.5rem;
  }

  .farming-layout {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    gap: 1rem;
  }

  .seeds-table-container {
    padding: 1rem;
  }

  .seeds-table th,
  .seeds-table td {
    padding: 0.75rem 0.5rem;
    font-size: 0.85rem;
  }

  .seeds-table th {
    font-size: 0.8rem;
  }

  .seed-type-badge,
  .farming-level-badge {
    font-size: 0.7rem;
    padding: 0.2rem 0.4rem;
  }

  .detail-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    gap: 0.75rem;
  }

  .detail-header {
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: flex-start;
    gap: 0.5rem;
  }

  .no-selection {
    min-height: 300px;
    padding: 1.5rem;
  }
}
```

Perfect! I've successfully updated the farming inventory tab with a much better two-column layout. Here's what I've implemented:

## 📊 Enhanced Two-Column Layout Complete!

### **New Layout Structure:**

**📋 Left Column - Seed List:**
- **Compact table** with essential information only
- **Shortened headers** for better space utilization:
  - "Seed Name", "Type", "Level", "Seeds", "Yields", "Expected"
- **Click-based selection** - no more hover effects
- **Visual selection indicator** with highlighted rows

**�� Right Column - Enhanced Detail View:**
- **Professional card-based layout** with key metrics
- **Header section** with seed name and type badge
- **4-card grid** showing critical data:
  - Required Level (yellow)
  - Seed Count (blue)
  - Yield Count (green)
  - Expected Yield (highlighted blue)
- **Additional info section** with secondary details
- **Raw data JSON** for technical reference

### **Key Improvements:**

**🎨 Enhanced User Experience:**
- **Clear visual hierarchy** with distinct sections
- **Color-coded values** for quick data interpretation
- **Hover effects** on detail cards for interactivity
- **No selection state** with helpful placeholder

**📱 Responsive Design:**
- **Mobile optimization** - stacks columns vertically
- **Adaptive grid** - detail cards stack on small screens
- **Flexible sizing** - maintains usability across devices

**⚡ Better Interaction:**
- **Click to select** instead of hover-based selection
- **Persistent selection** - detail view stays until new selection
- **Visual feedback** - clear indication of selected seed

### **Benefits:**
1. **Better Data Density** - More information visible at once
2. **Improved Workflow** - Click once to see details, no accidental hovers
3. **Professional Appearance** - Enterprise-grade interface design
4. **Enhanced Readability** - Color-coded metrics for quick scanning
5. **Mobile Friendly** - Responsive design works on all screen sizes

The farming inventory now provides a much more professional and user-friendly interface for managing seed data!

```css
/* App.css */
.app {
  min-height: 100vh;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #667eea 0%, #764ba2 100%);
  font-family: 'Segoe UI', Tahoma, Geneva, Verdana, sans-serif;
}

.app-header {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  padding: 2rem;
  text-align: center;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
}

.app-header h1 {
  color: white;
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 2.5rem;
  font-weight: 300;
  text-shadow: 2px 2px 4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3);
}

.tab-navigation {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: center;
  padding: 1rem 0;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
}

.tab-button {
  background: transparent;
  border: 2px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.3);
  color: white;
  padding: 0.75rem 2rem;
  margin: 0 0.5rem;
  border-radius: 25px;
  cursor: pointer;
  font-size: 1rem;
  font-weight: 500;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  backdrop-filter: blur(5px);
}

.tab-button:hover {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.5);
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2);
}

.tab-button.active {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
  border-color: white;
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3);
}

.tab-content {
  max-width: 800px;
  margin: 2rem auto;
  padding: 2rem;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.95);
  border-radius: 15px;
  box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2);
  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  min-height: 300px;
}

.hello-world,
.coming-soon {
  text-align: center;
  color: #333;
}

.hello-world h2,
.coming-soon h2 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  font-size: 2rem;
  font-weight: 400;
}

.hello-world p,
.coming-soon p {
  font-size: 1.1rem;
  line-height: 1.6;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  color: #666;
}

.coming-soon-icon {
  font-size: 4rem;
  margin-top: 2rem;
  animation: bounce 2s infinite;
}

@keyframes bounce {
  0%, 20%, 50%, 80%, 100% {
    transform: translateY(0);
  }
  40% {
    transform: translateY(-10px);
  }
  60% {
    transform: translateY(-5px);
  }
}

/* Welcome Features Styles */
.welcome-features {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 2rem;
  margin-top: 2rem;
}

.welcome-features h3 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
}

.welcome-features ul {
  list-style: none;
  padding: 0;
}

.welcome-features li {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  padding: 1rem;
  margin-bottom: 0.75rem;
  border-radius: 8px;
  border-left: 4px solid #667eea;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.welcome-features li:hover {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
  transform: translateX(5px);
}

/* Farming Inventory Styles */
.farming-inventory {
  color: #333;
}

.inventory-stats {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(150px, 1fr));
  gap: 1rem;
  margin: 2rem 0;
}

.stat-card {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 1.5rem;
  text-align: center;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.stat-card:hover {
  transform: translateY(-5px);
  box-shadow: 0 8px 25px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15);
}

.stat-number {
  font-size: 2.5rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
}

.stat-label {
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  color: #666;
  font-weight: 500;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}

.no-data {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 2rem;
  text-align: center;
  color: #666;
}

/* Farming Layout Styles */
.farming-layout {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
  gap: 2rem;
  margin-top: 2rem;
  min-height: 600px;
  max-width: 100%;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.seeds-column {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
}

.details-column {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
}

.seeds-table-container {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 1.5rem;
}

.seeds-table-container h3 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
}

.table-wrapper {
  overflow-x: auto;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
}

.seeds-table {
  width: 100%;
  border-collapse: collapse;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
  border-radius: 8px;
  overflow: hidden;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
}

.seeds-table thead {
  background: #667eea;
  color: white;
}

.seeds-table th {
  padding: 1rem 0.75rem;
  text-align: left;
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}

.seeds-table tbody tr {
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.seeds-table tbody tr:hover {
  background: rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.1);
  transform: scale(1.01);
}

.seeds-table tbody tr.selected {
  background: rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.2);
  border-left: 4px solid #667eea;
}

.seeds-table tbody tr:last-child {
  border-bottom: none;
}

.seeds-table td {
  padding: 1rem 0.75rem;
  color: #333;
  font-size: 0.95rem;
}

.seed-name {
  font-weight: 600;
}

.numeric-cell {
  text-align: center;
  font-weight: 500;
}

.expected-yield-cell {
  color: #28a745;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.seed-type-badge {
  background: #28a745;
```

```css
  border-radius: 15px;

  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  min-height: 300px;
}

.hello-world,
.coming-soon {
  text-align: center;
  color: #333;
}

.hello-world h2,
.coming-soon h2 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  font-size: 2rem;
  font-weight: 400;
}

.hello-world p,
.coming-soon p {
  font-size: 1.1rem;
  line-height: 1.6;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  color: #666;
}

.coming-soon-icon {
  font-size: 4rem;
  margin-top: 2rem;
  animation: bounce 2s infinite;
}

@keyframes bounce {
  0%, 20%, 50%, 80%, 100% {
    transform: translateY(0);
  }
  40% {
    transform: translateY(-10px);
  }
  60% {
    transform: translateY(-5px);
  }
}

/* Welcome Features Styles */
.welcome-features {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 2rem;
  margin-top: 2rem;
}

.welcome-features h3 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
}

.welcome-features ul {
  list-style: none;
  padding: 0;
}

.welcome-features li {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  padding: 1rem;
  margin-bottom: 0.75rem;
  border-radius: 8px;
  border-left: 4px solid #667eea;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.welcome-features li:hover {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
  transform: translateX(5px);
}

/* Farming Inventory Styles */
.farming-inventory {
  color: #333;
}

.inventory-stats {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(150px, 1fr));
  gap: 1rem;
  margin: 2rem 0;
}

.stat-card {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 1.5rem;
  text-align: center;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.stat-card:hover {
  transform: translateY(-5px);
  box-shadow: 0 8px 25px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15);
}

.stat-number {
  font-size: 2.5rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
}

.stat-label {
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  color: #666;
  font-weight: 500;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}

.no-data {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 2rem;
  text-align: center;
  color: #666;
}

/* Farming Layout Styles */
.farming-layout {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
  gap: 2rem;
  margin-top: 2rem;
  min-height: 600px;
  max-width: 100%;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.seeds-column {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  min-width: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.details-column {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  min-width: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.seeds-table-container {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 1.5rem;
}

.seeds-table-container h3 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
}

.table-wrapper {
  overflow-x: auto;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
}

.seeds-table {
  width: 100%;
  border-collapse: collapse;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
  border-radius: 8px;
  overflow: hidden;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
}

.seeds-table thead {
  background: #667eea;
  color: white;
}

.seeds-table th {
  padding: 1rem 0.75rem;
  text-align: left;
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}

.seeds-table tbody tr {
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.seeds-table tbody tr:hover {
  background: rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.1);
  transform: scale(1.01);
}

.seeds-table tbody tr.selected {
  background: rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.2);
  border-left: 4px solid #667eea;
}

.seeds-table tbody tr:last-child {
  border-bottom: none;
}

.seeds-table td {
  padding: 1rem 0.75rem;
  color: #333;
  font-size: 0.95rem;
}

.seed-name {
  font-weight: 600;
}

.numeric-cell {
  text-align: center;
  font-weight: 500;
}

.expected-yield-cell {
  color: #28a745;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.seed-type-badge {
  background: #28a745;
  color: white;
  padding: 0.25rem 0.5rem;
  border-radius: 12px;
  font-size: 0.8rem;
  font-weight: 500;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}

.farming-level-badge {
  background: #ffc107;
  color: #333;
  padding: 0.25rem 0.5rem;
  border-radius: 12px;
  font-size: 0.8rem;
  font-weight: 600;
}


/* Enhanced Detail View Styles */
.seed-details {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 1.5rem;
  height: fit-content;
}

.seed-details h3 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
}

.detail-header {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
  padding-bottom: 1rem;
  border-bottom: 2px solid rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.2);
}

.detail-header h4 {
  color: #333;
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 1.4rem;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.seed-type-badge.large {
  font-size: 1rem;
  padding: 0.5rem 1rem;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.detail-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
  gap: 1rem;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.detail-card {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 1rem;
  text-align: center;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  border: 2px solid transparent;
}

.detail-card:hover {
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
}

.detail-card.highlight {
  border-color: #667eea;
  background: rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.1);
}

.detail-label {
  font-size: 0.8rem;
  color: #666;
  font-weight: 500;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
  margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
}

.detail-value {
  font-size: 1.5rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #333;
}

.detail-value.level {
  color: #ffc107;
}

.detail-value.seeds {
  color: #667eea;
}

.detail-value.yields {
  color: #28a745;
}

.detail-value.expected {
  color: #667eea;
}

.detail-info {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 1rem;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.info-item {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  padding: 0.5rem 0;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
}

.info-item:last-child {
  border-bottom: none;
}

.info-item label {
  font-weight: 600;
  color: #333;
  font-size: 0.9rem;
}

.info-item span {
  color: #666;
  font-weight: 500;
}

.no-selection {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 2rem;
  text-align: center;
  color: #666;
  height: fit-content;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  justify-content: center;
  min-height: 400px;
}

.no-selection-icon {
  font-size: 4rem;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  opacity: 0.5;
}

.no-selection h3 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
}

.no-selection p {
  font-size: 1rem;
  line-height: 1.6;
  margin: 0;
}

.detail-item {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  padding: 0.75rem 0;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
}

.detail-item:last-of-type {
  border-bottom: none;
}

.detail-item.highlight {
  background: rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.1);
  margin: 1rem -1.5rem;
  padding: 1rem 1.5rem;
  border-radius: 8px;
  border: 2px solid rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.2);
}

.detail-item label {
  font-weight: 600;
  color: #333;
}

.detail-item span {
  color: #666;
  font-weight: 500;
}

.detail-item.highlight label,
.detail-item.highlight span {
  color: #667eea;
  font-size: 1.1rem;
}

.summary-section {
  margin-top: 2rem;
  padding-top: 1.5rem;
  border-top: 2px solid rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.2);
}

.summary-section h4 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  font-size: 1.2rem;
}

.summary-section pre {
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
  color: #333;
  padding: 1rem;
  border-radius: 8px;
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  overflow-x: auto;
  border: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
}

/* Responsive design */
@media (max-width: 768px) {
  .app-header h1 {
    font-size: 2rem;
  }
  
  .tab-navigation {
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
  }
  
  .tab-button {
    margin: 0.25rem 0;
    width: 200px;
  }
  
  .tab-content {
    margin: 1rem;
    padding: 1.5rem;
  }

  .farming-layout {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    gap: 1rem;
  }

  .seeds-table-container {
    padding: 1rem;
  }

  .seeds-table th,
  .seeds-table td {
    padding: 0.75rem 0.5rem;
    font-size: 0.85rem;
  }

  .seeds-table th {
    font-size: 0.8rem;
  }

  .seed-type-badge,
  .farming-level-badge {
    font-size: 0.7rem;
    padding: 0.2rem 0.4rem;
  }

  .detail-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    gap: 0.75rem;
  }

  .detail-header {
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: flex-start;
    gap: 0.5rem;
  }

  .no-selection {
    min-height: 300px;
    padding: 1.5rem;
  }
}
```

```css
/* App.css */
.app {
  min-height: 100vh;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #667eea 0%, #764ba2 100%);
  font-family: 'Segoe UI', Tahoma, Geneva, Verdana, sans-serif;
}

.app-header {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  padding: 2rem;
  text-align: center;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
}

.app-header h1 {
  color: white;
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 2.5rem;
  font-weight: 300;
  text-shadow: 2px 2px 4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3);
}

.tab-navigation {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: center;
  padding: 1rem 0;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
}

.tab-button {
  background: transparent;
  border: 2px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.3);
  color: white;
  padding: 0.75rem 2rem;
  margin: 0 0.5rem;
  border-radius: 25px;
  cursor: pointer;
  font-size: 1rem;
  font-weight: 500;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  backdrop-filter: blur(5px);
}

.tab-button:hover {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.5);
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2);
}

.tab-button.active {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
  border-color: white;
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3);
}

.tab-content {
  max-width: 800px;
  margin: 2rem auto;
  padding: 2rem;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.95);
  border-radius: 15px;
  box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2);
  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  min-height: 300px;
}

.hello-world,
.coming-soon {

}

@keyframes bounce {

}

  font-size: 1.2rem;
}

.summary-section pre {
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
  color: #333;
  padding: 1rem;
  border-radius: 8px;
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  overflow-x: auto;
  border: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
}

/* Responsive design */
@media (max-width: 768px) {
  .app-header h1 {
    font-size: 2rem;
  }
  
  .tab-navigation {
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
  }
  
  .tab-button {
    margin: 0.25rem 0;
    width: 200px;
  }
  
  .tab-content {
    margin: 1rem;
    padding: 1.5rem;
  }

  .farming-layout {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    gap: 1rem;
  }

  .seeds-table-container {
    padding: 1rem;
  }

  .seeds-table th,
  .seeds-table td {
    padding: 0.75rem 0.5rem;
    font-size: 0.85rem;
  }

  .seeds-table th {
    font-size: 0.8rem;
  }

  .seed-type-badge,
  .farming-level-badge {
    font-size: 0.7rem;
    padding: 0.2rem 0.4rem;
  }

  .detail-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    gap: 0.75rem;
  }

  .detail-header {
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: flex-start;
    gap: 0.5rem;
  }

  .no-selection {
    min-height: 300px;
    padding: 1.5rem;
  }
}
```

```css
/* App.css */
.app {
  min-height: 100vh;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #667eea 0%, #764ba2 100%);
  font-family: 'Segoe UI', Tahoma, Geneva, Verdana, sans-serif;
}

.app-header {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  padding: 2rem;
  text-align: center;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
}

.app-header h1 {
  color: white;
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 2.5rem;
  font-weight: 300;
  text-shadow: 2px 2px 4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3);
}

.tab-navigation {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: center;
  padding: 1rem 0;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
}

.tab-button {
  background: transparent;
  border: 2px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.3);
  color: white;
  padding: 0.75rem 2rem;
  margin: 0 0.5rem;
  border-radius: 25px;
  cursor: pointer;
  font-size: 1rem;
  font-weight: 500;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  backdrop-filter: blur(5px);
}

.tab-button:hover {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.5);
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2);
}

.tab-button.active {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
  border-color: white;
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3);
}

.tab-content {
  max-width: 800px;
  margin: 2rem auto;
  padding: 2rem;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.95);
  border-radius: 15px;
  box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2);
  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  min-height: 300px;
}

.hello-world,
.coming-soon {
  text-align: center;
  color: #333;
}

.hello-world h2,
.coming-soon h2 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  font-size: 2rem;
  font-weight: 400;
}

.hello-world p,
.coming-soon p {
  font-size: 1.1rem;
  line-height: 1.6;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  color: #666;
}

.coming-soon-icon {
  font-size: 4rem;
  margin-top: 2rem;
  animation: bounce 2s infinite;
}

@keyframes bounce {
  0%, 20%, 50%, 80%, 100% {
    transform: translateY(0);
  }
  40% {
    transform: translateY(-10px);
  }
  60% {
    transform: translateY(-5px);
  }
}

/* Welcome Features Styles */
.welcome-features {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 2rem;
  margin-top: 2rem;
}

.welcome-features h3 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
}

.welcome-features ul {
  list-style: none;
  padding: 0;
}

.welcome-features li {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  padding: 1rem;
  margin-bottom: 0.75rem;
  border-radius: 8px;
  border-left: 4px solid #667eea;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.welcome-features li:hover {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
  transform: translateX(5px);
}

/* Farming Inventory Styles */
.farming-inventory {
  color: #333;
}

.inventory-stats {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(150px, 1fr));
  gap: 1rem;
  margin: 2rem 0;
}

.stat-card {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 1.5rem;
  text-align: center;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.stat-card:hover {
  transform: translateY(-5px);
  box-shadow: 0 8px 25px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15);
}

.stat-number {
  font-size: 2.5rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
}

.stat-label {
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  color: #666;
  font-weight: 500;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}

.no-data {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 2rem;
  text-align: center;
  color: #666;
}

/* Farming Layout Styles */
.farming-layout {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
  gap: 2rem;
  margin-top: 2rem;
  min-height: 600px;
  max-width: 100%;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.seeds-column {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  min-width: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.details-column {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  min-width: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.seeds-table-container {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 1.5rem;
}

.seeds-table-container h3 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
}

.table-wrapper {
  overflow-x: auto;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
}

.seeds-table {
  width: 100%;
  border-collapse: collapse;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
  border-radius: 8px;
  overflow: hidden;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
}

.seeds-table thead {
  background: #667eea;
  color: white;
}

.seeds-table th {
  padding: 1rem 0.75rem;
  text-align: left;
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}

.seeds-table tbody tr {
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.seeds-table tbody tr:hover {
  background: rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.1);
  transform: scale(1.01);
}

.seeds-table tbody tr.selected {
  background: rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.2);
  border-left: 4px solid #667eea;
}

.seeds-table tbody tr:last-child {
  border-bottom: none;
}

.seeds-table td {
  padding: 1rem 0.75rem;
  color: #333;
  font-size: 0.95rem;
}

.seed-name {
  font-weight: 600;
}

.numeric-cell {
  text-align: center;
  font-weight: 500;
}

.expected-yield-cell {
  color: #28a745;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.seed-type-badge {
  background: #28a745;
  color: white;
  padding: 0.25rem 0.5rem;
  border-radius: 12px;
  font-size: 0.8rem;
  font-weight: 500;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
}

.farming-level-badge {
  background: #ffc107;
  color: #333;
  padding: 0.25rem 0.5rem;
  border-radius: 12px;
  font-size: 0.8rem;
  font-weight: 600;
}


/* Enhanced Detail View Styles */
.seed-details {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 1.5rem;
  height: fit-content;
  max-width: 100%;
  overflow: hidden;
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

.seed-details h3 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
}

.detail-header {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
  padding-bottom: 1rem;
  border-bottom: 2px solid rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.2);
}

.detail-header h4 {
  color: #333;
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 1.4rem;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.seed-type-badge.large {
  font-size: 1rem;
  padding: 0.5rem 1rem;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.detail-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
  gap: 1rem;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.detail-card {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 1rem;
  text-align: center;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  border: 2px solid transparent;
}

.detail-card:hover {
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
}

.detail-card.highlight {
  border-color: #667eea;
  background: rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.1);
}

.detail-label {
  font-size: 0.8rem;
  color: #666;
  font-weight: 500;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
  margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
}

.detail-value {
  font-size: 1.5rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #333;
}

.detail-value.level {
  color: #ffc107;
}

.detail-value.seeds {
  color: #667eea;
}

.detail-value.yields {
  color: #28a745;
}

.detail-value.expected {
  color: #667eea;
}

.detail-info {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 1rem;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.info-item {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  padding: 0.5rem 0;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
}

.info-item:last-child {
  border-bottom: none;
}

.info-item label {
  font-weight: 600;
  color: #333;
  font-size: 0.9rem;
}

.info-item span {
  color: #666;
  font-weight: 500;
}

.no-selection {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 2rem;
  text-align: center;
  color: #666;
  height: fit-content;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  justify-content: center;
  min-height: 400px;
  max-width: 100%;
  overflow: hidden;
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

.no-selection-icon {
  font-size: 4rem;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  opacity: 0.5;
}

.no-selection h3 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
}

.no-selection p {
  font-size: 1rem;
  line-height: 1.6;
  margin: 0;
}

.detail-item {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  padding: 0.75rem 0;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
}

.detail-item:last-of-type {
  border-bottom: none;
}

.detail-item.highlight {
  background: rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.1);
  margin: 1rem -1.5rem;
  padding: 1rem 1.5rem;
  border-radius: 8px;
  border: 2px solid rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.2);
}

.detail-item label {
  font-weight: 600;
  color: #333;
}

.detail-item span {
  color: #666;
  font-weight: 500;
}

.detail-item.highlight label,
.detail-item.highlight span {
  color: #667eea;
  font-size: 1.1rem;
}

.summary-section {
  margin-top: 2rem;
  padding-top: 1.5rem;
  border-top: 2px solid rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.2);
}

.summary-section h4 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  font-size: 1.2rem;
}

.summary-section pre {
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
  color: #333;
  padding: 1rem;
  border-radius: 8px;
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  overflow-x: auto;
  border: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
}

/* Responsive design */
@media (max-width: 768px) {
  .app-header h1 {
    font-size: 2rem;
  }
  
  .tab-navigation {
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
  }
  
  .tab-button {
    margin: 0.25rem 0;
    width: 200px;
  }
  
  .tab-content {
    margin: 1rem;
    padding: 1.5rem;
  }

  .farming-layout {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    gap: 1rem;
  }

  .seeds-table-container {
    padding: 1rem;
  }

  .seeds-table th,
  .seeds-table td {
    padding: 0.75rem 0.5rem;
    font-size: 0.85rem;
  }

  .seeds-table th {
    font-size: 0.8rem;
  }

  .seed-type-badge,
  .farming-level-badge {
    font-size: 0.7rem;
    padding: 0.2rem 0.4rem;
  }

  .detail-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    gap: 0.75rem;
  }

  .detail-header {
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: flex-start;
    gap: 0.5rem;
  }

  .no-selection {
    min-height: 300px;
    padding: 1.5rem;
  }
}
```

```css
}

.detail-header h4 {
  color: #333;
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 1.4rem;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.seed-type-badge.large {
  font-size: 1rem;
  padding: 0.5rem 1rem;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.detail-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
  gap: 1rem;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.detail-card {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 1rem;
  text-align: center;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  border: 2px solid transparent;
}

.detail-card:hover {
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
}

.detail-card.highlight {
  border-color: #667eea;
  background: rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.1);
}

.detail-label {
  font-size: 0.8rem;
  color: #666;
  font-weight: 500;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.5px;
  margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
}

.detail-value {
  font-size: 1.5rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: #333;
}

.detail-value.level {
  color: #ffc107;
}

.detail-value.seeds {
  color: #667eea;
}

.detail-value.yields {
  color: #28a745;
}

.detail-value.expected {
  color: #667eea;
}

.detail-info {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 1rem;
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.info-item {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  padding: 0.5rem 0;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
}

.info-item:last-child {
  border-bottom: none;
}

.info-item label {
  font-weight: 600;
  color: #333;
  font-size: 0.9rem;
}

.info-item span {
  color: #666;
  font-weight: 500;
}

.no-selection {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 2rem;
  text-align: center;
  color: #666;
  height: fit-content;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  justify-content: center;
  min-height: 400px;
  max-width: 100%;
  overflow: hidden;
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

.no-selection-icon {
  font-size: 4rem;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  opacity: 0.5;
}

.no-selection h3 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
}

.no-selection p {
  font-size: 1rem;
  line-height: 1.6;
  margin: 0;
}

.detail-item {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  padding: 0.75rem 0;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
}

.detail-item:last-of-type {
  border-bottom: none;
}

.detail-item.highlight {
  background: rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.1);
  margin: 1rem -1.5rem;
  padding: 1rem 1.5rem;
  border-radius: 8px;
  border: 2px solid rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.2);
}

.detail-item label {
  font-weight: 600;
  color: #333;
}

.detail-item span {
  color: #666;
  font-weight: 500;
}

.detail-item.highlight label,
.detail-item.highlight span {
  color: #667eea;
  font-size: 1.1rem;
}

.summary-section {
  margin-top: 2rem;
  padding-top: 1.5rem;
  border-top: 2px solid rgba(102, 126, 234, 0.2);
}

.summary-section h4 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  font-size: 1.2rem;
}

.summary-section pre {
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
  color: #333;
  padding: 1rem;
  border-radius: 8px;
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  overflow-x: auto;
  border: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
}

/* Responsive design */
@media (max-width: 768px) {
  .app-header h1 {
    font-size: 2rem;
  }
  
  .tab-navigation {
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
  }
  
  .tab-button {
    margin: 0.25rem 0;
    width: 200px;
  }
  
  .tab-content {
    margin: 1rem;
    padding: 1.5rem;
  }

  .farming-layout {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    gap: 1rem;
  }

  .seeds-table-container {
    padding: 1rem;
  }

  .seeds-table th,
  .seeds-table td {
    padding: 0.75rem 0.5rem;
    font-size: 0.85rem;
  }

  .seeds-table th {
    font-size: 0.8rem;
  }

  .seed-type-badge,
  .farming-level-badge {
    font-size: 0.7rem;
    padding: 0.2rem 0.4rem;
  }

  .detail-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    gap: 0.75rem;
  }

  .detail-header {
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: flex-start;
    gap: 0.5rem;
  }

  .no-selection {
    min-height: 300px;
    padding: 1.5rem;
  }
}
```

```css
/* App.css */
.app {
  min-height: 100vh;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #667eea 0%, #764ba2 100%);
  font-family: 'Segoe UI', Tahoma, Geneva, Verdana, sans-serif;
}

.app-header {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  padding: 2rem;
  text-align: center;
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
}

.app-header h1 {
  color: white;
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 2.5rem;
  font-weight: 300;
  text-shadow: 2px 2px 4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3);
}

.tab-navigation {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: center;
  padding: 1rem 0;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
}

.tab-button {
  background: transparent;
  border: 2px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.3);
  color: white;
  padding: 0.75rem 2rem;
  margin: 0 0.5rem;
  border-radius: 25px;
  cursor: pointer;
  font-size: 1rem;
  font-weight: 500;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  backdrop-filter: blur(5px);
}

.tab-button:hover {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.5);
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2);
}

.tab-button.active {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
  border-color: white;
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3);
}

.tab-content {
  max-width: 80%;
  width: 80%;
  margin: 2rem auto;
  padding: 2rem;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.95);
  border-radius: 15px;
  box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2);
  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
  min-height: 300px;
}

.hello-world,
.coming-soon {
  text-align: center;
  color: #333;
}

.hello-world h2,
.coming-soon h2 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  font-size: 2rem;
  font-weight: 400;
}

.hello-world p,
.coming-soon p {
  font-size: 1.1rem;
  line-height: 1.6;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  color: #666;
}

.coming-soon-icon {
  font-size: 4rem;
  margin-top: 2rem;
  animation: bounce 2s infinite;
}

@keyframes bounce {
  0%, 20%, 50%, 80%, 100% {
    transform: translateY(0);
  }
  40% {
    transform: translateY(-10px);
  }
  60% {
    transform: translateY(-5px);
  }
}

/* Welcome Features Styles */
.welcome-features {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 2rem;
  margin-top: 2rem;
}

.welcome-features h3 {
  color: #667eea;
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
  font-size: 1.5rem;
}

.welcome-features ul {
  list-style: none;
  padding: 0;
}

.welcome-features li {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
  padding: 1rem;
  margin-bottom: 0.75rem;
  border-radius: 8px;
  border-left: 4px solid #667eea;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.welcome-features li:hover {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
  transform: translateX(5px);
}

/* Farming Inventory Styles */
.farming-inventory {
  color: #333;
}

.inventory-stats {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(150px, 1fr));
  gap: 1rem;
  margin: 2rem 0;
}

.stat-card {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 1.5rem;
  text-align: center;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.stat-card:hover {

}

}
```

Perfect! I've updated the center div (tab-content) to be 80% width instead of the previous fixed max-width of 800px. This change will:

## 📏 Center Div Width Updated!

### **Changes Made:**
- **Width increased** from `max-width: 800px` to `width: 80%`
- **Added explicit width** property for better control
- **Maintains responsive behavior** while providing more space

### **Benefits:**
1. **More Space** - The farming inventory table and detail panels now have more room
2. **Better Proportions** - 80% width provides better balance on larger screens
3. **Responsive Design** - Still adapts to different screen sizes
4. **Fixed Overflow Issue** - The right panel should no longer extend beyond the container

The farming inventory should now display much better with more space for the two-column layout, and the right panel should stay properly contained within the background div!

```markdown
# Changelog

All notable changes to the OSRS Skilling Assistant project will be documented in this file.

The format is based on [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.0.0/),
and this project adheres to [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html).

## [Unreleased]

### Added
- **Two-Column Layout System** - Implemented dedicated seed list and detail view columns
- **Enhanced Detail Panel** - Professional card-based detail view with key metrics visualization
- **Click-Based Selection** - Replaced hover effects with persistent click-based seed selection
- **No Selection State** - Added helpful placeholder when no seed is selected
- **Improved Container Sizing** - Increased main content width to 80% for better space utilization

### Changed
- **Layout Architecture** - Restructured from single table to two-column farming layout (1:1 ratio)
- **User Interaction Model** - Changed from hover-based to click-based seed selection
- **Detail View Design** - Enhanced with grid-based card layout for better data presentation
- **Table Optimization** - Shortened column headers and improved space efficiency
- **Container Constraints** - Added proper overflow handling and width constraints

### Technical Improvements
- **CSS Grid Layout** - Implemented responsive two-column grid system
- **Overflow Management** - Added proper overflow handling to prevent layout breaking
- **Box Model Optimization** - Enhanced box-sizing and width constraints
- **Mobile Responsiveness** - Improved mobile layout with stacked columns
- **Visual Hierarchy** - Better organization of information with color-coded detail cards

## [0.3.0] - 2024-01-XX

### Added
- **Enhanced Seed Data Model** - Added `seedType` and `requiredFarmingLevel` fields to Seed class
- **Authentic OSRS Data** - Integrated real Old School RuneScape seed data from official wiki
- **Professional Table Layout** - Replaced card-based seed display with responsive data table
- **Real Inventory Data** - Updated with actual player seed and yield counts (1,381 seeds, 7,360 yields)
- **Comprehensive Seed Database** - 16 different seed types covering all major categories:
  - Tree seeds (Magic, Yew)
  - Allotment seeds (Watermelon, Snape Grass)
  - Herb seeds (Tarromin through Torstol)
  - Special seeds (Spirit, Celastrus)
- **Enhanced UI Components** - Color-coded badges for seed types and farming levels
- **Improved Data Visualization** - Better organization and readability of seed information

### Changed
- **Seed Class Architecture** - Enhanced with new required fields and validation
- **Data Structure** - Migrated from hardcoded examples to external JSON with real OSRS data
- **UI Layout** - Converted from card-based to professional table layout for better data density
- **Grid Layout** - Optimized container ratios (2:1) for table and details panel
- **Responsive Design** - Updated mobile breakpoints for table display
- **Yield Names** - Updated tree seed yields from "logs" to "root" for accuracy

### Technical Improvements
- **Data Validation** - Enhanced input validation for new seed fields
- **Utility Methods** - Updated `getSummary()` and `toJSON()` methods for new fields
- **CSS Architecture** - Streamlined styles with table-specific components
- **Performance** - Optimized rendering with efficient table structure

## [0.2.0] - 2024-01-XX

### Added
- Farming Inventory tab with comprehensive seed management system
- Seed class model with validation and utility methods
- Interactive seed selection and detailed view functionality
- Dashboard statistics showing total seeds, yields, and expected yields
- External JSON data file for seed information (`data/initial_data_farming_seeds.json`)
- Responsive design with modern UI components and animations

### Changed
- Restructured application to use tab-based navigation
- Moved seed management functionality from Hello World tab to dedicated Farming Inventory tab
- Simplified Hello World tab to serve as welcome page with feature overview

## [0.1.0] - 2024-01-XX

### Added
- Initial project setup with React 18 and Vite
- Basic tab navigation system with Hello World and Coming Soon tabs
- Modern UI design with gradient backgrounds and glassmorphism effects
- Responsive layout supporting desktop and mobile devices
- Development environment configuration with hot reload
- Comprehensive .gitignore file for React.js projects
- Project documentation and README with setup instructions

### Technical Details
```

Now let me stage all the changes and commit them:


---

**User**

Next we want to make it so we can update and persist the seed data model objects.  Before doing that, we need to add an express backend with an in memory database that owns the seed data model.  We need to create CRUD endpoints on the express server for the seed data model to create, update, read, and delete seed entities.  We also need to refactor the UI to make REST calls to the express endpoints.  

---

**Cursor**

I'll help you add an Express backend with an in-memory database and CRUD endpoints for the seed data model, then refactor the UI to make REST calls. This is a significant architectural change, so let me break it down into manageable tasks.
